# SPAWN-VIABILITY AUDIT - 2026-10-02 (read-only, static)

**Question (Abs0lum):** "First, make sure there aren't any mobs that WON'T spawn in our world due to custom biomes or conditions of any kind." Plus his witness observation: **"very little aquatic mobs"** in game - treated as TRUE; this audit looks for causes.

**Scope:** step 1 only - can each mob's natural spawn rule ever fire in a NEW world made with the current packs? No habitat research, nothing changed. Packs read: BP-02 1.3.205 (`/home/claude/_build/bp02-205`), PW-StripMine BP 1.3.13 (`/home/claude/_build/stripmine-bp-1313`), BP-01 1.3.35 / BP-03 1.3.34 (`bp01-135`, `bp03-134` - scripts only, no entities/biomes/features), vanilla = Mojang bedrock-samples **1.26.50.4** (the local checkout is sparse; its `behavior_pack/biomes`, `spawn_rules`, `entities` and `metadata/vanilladata_modules/mojang-blocks.json` were exported read-only from git HEAD into the scratchpad).

> **Static is not runtime (P1).** This audit can only rule spawns OUT or flag risk. It cannot prove that any mob DOES spawn - only Abs0lum's in-game witness confirms that. Tool already in the pack for that: `/scriptevent sf:spawntest <entity id>` (StripMine `scripts/entity/SpawnRulesTester.js`, imported by `scripts/main.js`) prints every natural spawn of that id with its coordinates and biome; `/scriptevent sf:spawntest stop` ends it, `tp_last_found` teleports to the newest one.

## 0. Plain-language summary

### Counts

| | entities |
|---|---|
| Total entity definitions (all packs, unique identifiers) | **739** (vanilla 128 - 31 of them overridden by BP-02 for size only; BP-02 own 3; StripMine 608) |
| ...that are real creatures (not projectiles, eggs, props, boats, utility) | 663 |
| With a natural spawn rule | **609** |
| VIABLE (can fire somewhere in a new world) | **570** |
| AT RISK (can fire, but only conditionally / rarely / crowded / shrunk habitat) | **30** |
| NOT VIABLE (can never fire) | **7** |
| UNDECIDED (depends on game version) | **2** |
| No spawn rule at all | **130** (not a creature (projectile, egg, prop, utility) 76; vanilla mob (engine/structure-spawned) 33; creature without natural spawn 21) |

By pack: BP-02 1.3.205 AT RISK 4; BP-02 1.3.205 NO RULE 8; BP-02 1.3.205 NOT VIABLE 1; BP-02 1.3.205 VIABLE 21; StripMine 1.3.13 AT RISK 26; StripMine 1.3.13 NO RULE 58; StripMine 1.3.13 NOT VIABLE 6; StripMine 1.3.13 VIABLE 518; vanilla NO RULE 64; vanilla UNDECIDED 2; vanilla VIABLE 31.

### What this means in plain words

- **Our custom biomes do NOT stop any mob completely.** No mob lost its only home to a custom biome. The worst case is the vanilla **goat**: `pw:alpine_peak` replaces 100% of Frozen Peaks and Jagged Peaks, and it does not carry the `frozen_peaks` / `jagged_peaks` tags the goat rule asks for, so goats are left with the 60% of Snowy Slopes that `pw:snowy_taiga_sparse` did not take. Five AnF animals that ask for the vanilla `bamboo` tag lose 70% of their only biome because `pw:emerald_jungle` carries `bamboo_jungle` but not `bamboo`.
- **The real blockers are inside the spawn rules themselves** (mostly third-party StripMine ports): tags no biome has (e.g. `deep_ocean`, `swampland`, `badlands`, `old_growth_birch_forest`, all Biomes O' Plenty `bop_ks:*` tags), filters that demand several biome tags no biome has together (e.g. "jungle AND river"), and a desert rule that asks for grass blocks (`pw:ostrich_wa`).
- **Wooded Badlands collision:** `minecraft:mesa_plateau_stone` is claimed by both of our badlands biomes; the vanilla wolf (conditions[7]) and armadillo (conditions[2]) conditions for Wooded Badlands need tags `plateau`/`stone` that neither replacement carries - those two conditions are UNDECIDED (both mobs still spawn elsewhere).
- **Our ground scripts do not replace the surface in a new world by default.** `pw:grass_block` / `pw:podzol` / `pw:mycelium` only appear after `/scriptevent pw:groundconvert on` (riser conversion, off by default) or while the PW-SlabForge key pack is loaded (slab smoother). Even then, BP-02 already overrides the 12 vanilla grass rules and 252 StripMine block filters to list `pw:grass_block` too.
- **Many conditions inside otherwise-working rules are dead** (they cost nothing, but that part of the habitat never happens) - 163 dead conditions, see section 3.3.

Dead conditions inside rules that still work elsewhere: Biomes O' Plenty (bop_ks:*) tags - that mod is not installed **96**; vanilla-sounding tag no biome carries (typo / legacy biome name) **51**; contradictory AND filter **8**; block filter names no surface block of the matched biome(s) (e.g. grass in snowy grove/desert) **8**.

### Aquatic findings in plain words (why so few water mobs)

- **Two invisible hogs take most ocean spawns of the "animal" group.** In every ocean the "animal" population (where vanilla squid, dolphins, glow squid, nautilus and almost all of our sharks, whales, rays, orcas and seals live) has 25 competing rules with total weight 772; `pw:shrimp_wa` (weight 300, herds of 3-19, ~0.25 blocks long) and `pw:swordfish_wa` (weight 300) take **78%** of every pick. Vanilla squid drops from roughly 1 in 6 picks in a vanilla world (1 in 3 if his version has no nautilus) to about 1 in 100.
- **They never leave.** 57 spawn-rule mobs have **no `minecraft:despawn` component** (Bedrock then never despawns them): 12 sea creatures (`anaconda_ws`, `giant_squid_wwa`, `hammerhead_shark_wa`, `lantern_fish_wa`, `orca_wa`, `shark_wa`, `shrimp_wa`, `stingray_wa`, `swordfish_wa`, `tiger_shark_wa`, `whale_wa`, `white_shark_wa`) and 45 land animals (all WA ports, several WS, `pw:boar_ifs`). Every one that spawns stays forever and keeps counting against the population cap around the player; once the cap near the player is full, nothing new in that population spawns - land or sea. (8 AnF land animals also carry `minecraft:persistent`.)
- **Their "not in deep ocean" exclusion does nothing**: shrimp and swordfish exclude tag `deep_ocean`, which no biome has, so they flood deep oceans too.
- **Most of our sea creatures are filed as "animal", not "water_animal"** (WA/WWA/YTri whales, sharks, rays, orcas, jellyfish, crabs, morays, seals) - they compete with ~120-175 land-animal rules for one cap, and only vanilla cod/salmon/tropical fish/pufferfish/axolotl plus the Naturalist (sf_nba) fish use the separate water_animal cap.
- **Several AnF sea animals can never be in the water:** `shark_greatwhite/hammerhead/lemon/tiger_anf`, `octopus_blueringed/pacificgiant_anf`, `eagle_sea_anf` only have `spawns_on_surface` with an ocean-only filter -> they can only appear on islands inside an ocean biome.
- **Dead aquatic conditions:** `sf_nba:electric_eel` (only tag `deep_ocean` -> NOT VIABLE), `sf_nba:bass` and `sf_nba:catfish` swamp conditions (tag `swampland` does not exist; catfish keeps only mangrove swamps), every `bop_ks:` bayou/bog/wetland condition (bass, catfish, otter, platypus, piranha, giant salamander, kiwi).
- **Ponds and lakes get almost nothing:** a pond takes the tag of the land biome around it; apart from jungle (piranha, river dolphin, manatee, otter, gavial, kiwi) and the underground cavefish/glow squid, no fish rule accepts a land biome.
- **Our biomes do not touch the sea.** No ocean or beach is replaced; `pw:misty_river` and `pw:swamp_lagoon` keep the `river` / `swamp` tags the rules use. No sea-floor block filter exists in any water rule, so sea-floor materials do not block anything. The few-fish cause is the StripMine ocean rules and missing despawn, not our biomes.
- Vanilla fish (cod, salmon, tropical fish, pufferfish) still have their vanilla rules (BP-02 changes only their size); in the water_animal pool they now share with up to 18 StripMine rules - cod falls from ~74% to ~30% of picks in temperate/cold oceans, and slots go to small bottom creatures (clam, starfish, giant isopod, octopus) that are easy to overlook.

### Biomes a new world can contain (short)

- **75 biomes** can generate: 49 vanilla overworld (+2 version-dependent: dappled_forest, sulfur_caves), 5 Nether, 1 End, and **18 of our 19 custom biomes**: alpine_meadow, alpine_peak, amber_wooded_badlands, badlands_mesa, boreal_forest, cherry_valley, coastal_bluff, desert_dunes, emerald_jungle, forest_sparse, icy_cliffs, misty_dark_forest, misty_river, old_growth_pines, pale_hills, river_canyon, snowy_taiga_sparse, swamp_lagoon.
- **Never generated:** `pw:taiga_sparse` (it only targets `mega_taiga_hills` and `redwood_taiga_hills_mutated`, legacy ids the 1.18+ generator no longer places).
- **Vanilla biomes gone from a new world:** minecraft:cherry_grove (fully replaced (amount 1.0) by pw:cherry_valley); minecraft:frozen_peaks (fully replaced (amount 1.0) by pw:alpine_peak); minecraft:jagged_peaks (fully replaced (amount 1.0) by pw:alpine_peak); minecraft:mesa_plateau_stone (COLLISION: claimed by pw:amber_wooded_badlands (0.85), pw:ba); minecraft:pale_garden (fully replaced (amount 1.0) by pw:pale_hills); pw:taiga_sparse (targets only legacy (never-placed) vanilla ids: minecraft:me).
- **Collision:** `minecraft:mesa_plateau_stone` (Wooded Badlands) is claimed by BOTH `pw:amber_wooded_badlands` (0.85) and `pw:badlands_mesa` (0.90) - Lesson #135 collision audit hit; which one wins and whether any vanilla sliver survives is not knowable statically (and the standing engine-law note says the tie-break claim in #135 itself needs verification).
- Water: oceans (warm, lukewarm, temperate, cold, frozen + deep variants of all but warm), rivers (`river` 40% + `pw:misty_river` 60%, `frozen_river`), swamps (`swampland` 40% + `pw:swamp_lagoon` 60%, `mangrove_swamp`), beaches (sand, snowy, stony), cave water (lush caves, dripstone, deep dark). **No lake biome exists in Bedrock and none of our features creates lakes**; inland ponds/aquifer pools take the tags of the land biome around them.
- Full table with every tag: section 1.

### Surface blocks our packs replace (short)

- **Default new world: none.** No feature or script swaps a vanilla surface/sea-floor block for a pw: block on its own. Custom biomes only choose different *vanilla* materials (e.g. `pw:icy_cliffs` top = packed ice, `pw:boreal_forest` top = podzol, `pw:river_canyon` top = red sand) - those are evaluated per rule in section 3.
- **Opt-in:** `/scriptevent pw:groundconvert on` -> `scripts/pw_ground.js` `pwConvertRiser` turns `minecraft:grass_block` / `podzol` / `mycelium` at slab risers into `pw:` versions, which then spread onto lit dirt by random tick. PW-SlabForge key pack present -> `scripts/main.js` slab smoother (`PW_SLAB_MAP`, 36 vanilla ground blocks) puts `pw:slab_*` bottom slabs on terrace lips. No spawn rule lists any `pw:slab_*` block, so a lip column with a slab on top cannot host a block-filtered spawn (hairline effect).
- Our tree features cap custom forests with `pw:*_leaves`; leaf-filtered rules use the legacy ids `minecraft:leaves/leaves2/log/log2` (section 3.4), which match neither vanilla nor pw leaves today.

## 1. Biomes of a new world

### 1.1 How the 19 custom biomes enter world generation

All 19 files (`bp02-205/biomes/*.biome.json`) are `format_version` **1.21.110** and use **`minecraft:replace_biomes`** only (no `overworld_generation_rules`). Each replaces a noise-chosen share (`amount`) of the listed vanilla biomes; the rest of the vanilla biome remains. Experimental toggle: nothing in the files or the pack manifest names an experiment and the 1.26.50 docs (`metadata/doc_modules/biomes.json`) do not mark `replace_biomes` experimental - **UNDECIDED statically**; if his world ever needed a "Custom Biomes" toggle and it were off, none of the 19 would apply and every spawn would follow the vanilla tags (that case only removes the custom-biome effects listed here).

### 1.4 Water bodies and lakes

- Oceans: `ocean`, `deep_ocean` (temperate); `warm_ocean`; `lukewarm_ocean`, `deep_lukewarm_ocean`; `cold_ocean`, `deep_cold_ocean`; `frozen_ocean`, `deep_frozen_ocean`. None touched by our packs. Note the vanilla tags: deep oceans carry `deep` + `ocean` - there is **no `deep_ocean` tag**; lukewarm oceans carry `lukewarm` (not `warm`).
- Rivers: `minecraft:river` (40% left) + `pw:misty_river` (60%, tags `river overworld monster animal pw_river pw_misty pw_trees_sparse` - it drops `spawns_river_mobs`, `spawns_more_frequent_drowned`, `spawns_reduced_water_ambient_mobs`, which no spawn-rule file references; their engine meaning is not documented in the samples), `frozen_river` (untouched).
- Swamps: `swampland` (40% left) + `pw:swamp_lagoon` (60%, drops `slime`, `spawns_slimes_on_surface`, `swamp_water_huge_mushroom`), `mangrove_swamp` (untouched). Vanilla has **no fish rule for plain swamps** at all.
- Beaches: `beach`, `cold_beach`, `stone_beach` (untouched). Cave water: `lush_caves`, `dripstone_caves`, `deep_dark`.
- **Lakes:** Bedrock has no lake biome. None of our 89 features / 26 feature rules places a water body (`pw:frozen_waterfall_feature` only uses water as a replace target in `pw:icy_cliffs`). Lakes/ponds in a new world come from vanilla terrain (sea-level basins, aquifers); a pond is part of the land biome around it, so only rules that accept that land biome AND allow `spawns_underwater` can fill it - in practice almost no fish (see section 5).

## 2. Surface blocks of a new world

| mechanism | file | what it changes | active in a new world? |
|---|---|---|---|
| custom biome surface builders | `bp02-205/biomes/*.biome.json` `minecraft:surface_builder` + `minecraft:surface_material_adjustments` | vanilla materials per biome (table 1.3) | yes (where the biome generates) |
| riser conversion | `bp02-205/scripts/pw_ground.js` `pwConvertRiser()` (gate `pw:ground_convert_on`, default false) | `minecraft:grass_block`/`podzol`/`mycelium` -> `pw:grass_block`/`pw:podzol`/`pw:mycelium` at slab risers | **no** - only after `/scriptevent pw:groundconvert on` |
| pw ground spread | `pw_ground.js` `onRandomTick()` | an existing `pw:grass_block`/`pw:mycelium` spreads onto lit dirt | only once some pw ground exists |
| slab smoother | `bp02-205/scripts/main.js` `PW_SLAB_MAP` (lines ~4329-4346), gate "SLABFORGE" (`_forgeBeat`) | places `pw:slab_*` bottom slabs above 36 vanilla ground blocks on terrace lips | **no** - only while the PW-SlabForge key pack heartbeats |
| snow caps | `main.js` (`pw:snowcap_stairs`, `pw:snowcap_slab`, `pw:seated_snow`) | overlay caps on vanilla stairs/slabs/snow | part of the smoother pass |
| our features | `bp02-205/feature_rules/*.json` | only vanilla blocks (moss, gravel, clay, sand, granite, packed ice...) and `pw:*_leaves` trees | yes |
| BP-01 / BP-03 / StripMine scripts | - | no terrain conversion (StripMine scripts only place their own egg/ant-hill blocks on player action) | - |

Rules that list `minecraft:grass_block` but not `pw:grass_block`: 6 conditions (would miss converted risers only if conversion is switched on - hairline effect). Every BP-02 override and 252 StripMine filters already list both.

## 3. Every spawn rule

Method (all in `scratchpad/spawnaudit/`): `collect.py` parses every `entities/` and `spawn_rules/` file (tolerant JSON: comments + trailing commas; 0 parse failures, 77 files needed the tolerant pass), `world_model.py` builds the biome list, `analyze.py` evaluates each condition against every biome of a new world (3-valued: yes / only-conditionally / no) - biome_filter (all_of/any_of/none_of, `==`/`!=`/`not`), placement (surface / underground / underwater / lava vs. the biome's water class), height_filter vs. the placement's y-band, brightness for underground, spawns_on_block_filter / spawns_above_block_filter vs. the biome's surface/sea-floor blocks (ids checked against `mojang-blocks.json` 1.26.50 + our 467 custom blocks), weight, density_limit, permute_type, spawn_event, and the entity side (`minecraft:entity_spawned` simulated through add/trigger/sequence/randomize for `instant_despawn`/`transformation`, short timers, StripMine `DespawnRule.js`). `verdict.py` aggregates; `competition.py` sums weights per biome and population; `report.py` writes this file.

### 3.2 Biome tags in spawn filters that no biome of a new world carries

The `minecraft:sane` class of error. A positive test on such a tag can never pass; a `!=` test on it excludes nothing (the intended exclusion silently does not happen).

| tag | test | tag tests (count) | rules (examples) | intended / fix |

| `bop_ks:* (Biomes O' Plenty)` | == | 365 | `sf_nba:alligator`, `sf_nba:anteater`, `sf_nba:badger`, `sf_nba:bass`, `sf_nba:beetle` +76 | that mod is not installed - remove, or map to a vanilla/pw tag |
| `sunflower_plains` | == | 12 | `minecraft:bee`, `pw:butterfly_crimsonrose_anf`, `pw:butterfly_monarch_anf`, `pw:caracal_wa`, `pw:caterpillar_crimsonrose_anf` +7 | plains + mutated |
| `frozen_river` | != | 11 | `sf_nba:beaver`, `sf_nba:capybara`, `sf_nba:otter`, `sf_nba:platypus` | no-op exclusion; intended: != frozen + river |
| `birch_forest` | == | 9 | `pw:newt_crested_anf`, `sf_nba:cardinal`, `sf_nba:deer`, `sf_nba:duck`, `sf_nba:goose` +4 | birch |

### 3.3 Dead conditions inside rules that still work elsewhere

| cause | conditions | examples |
|---|---|---|
| Biomes O' Plenty (bop_ks:*) tags - that mod is not installed | 96 | sf_nba:alligator c2, sf_nba:anteater c1, sf_nba:badger c4, sf_nba:bass c3, sf_nba:beetle c6, sf_nba:beetle c7 |
| vanilla-sounding tag no biome carries (typo / legacy biome name) | 51 | pw:caracal_wa c1, pw:dove_wa c1, pw:dove_wa c2, pw:saiga_wwa c1, sf_nba:badger c1, sf_nba:bass c0 |
| contradictory AND filter | 8 | pw:boar_wa c0, pw:cougar_wa c0, pw:crocodile_wa c0, pw:deer_wa c0, pw:gorilla_wa c0, pw:hedgehog_wa c0 |
| block filter names no surface block of the matched biome(s) (e.g. grass in snowy grove/desert) | 8 | pw:komodo_dragon_wa c0, sf_nba:bluejay c3, sf_nba:cardinal c3, sf_nba:cardinal c4, sf_nba:cardinal c6, sf_nba:crow c3 |


UNDECIDED conditions inside working rules (7): minecraft:armadillo c2 ((mesa AND plateau)); minecraft:creeper c0 ((monster AND sulfur_caves)); minecraft:fox c2 (dappled_forest); minecraft:skeleton c0 ((monster AND sulfur_caves)); minecraft:witch c0 ((monster AND sulfur_caves)); minecraft:wolf c7 ((mesa AND plateau AND stone)); minecraft:zombie c0 ((monster AND sulfur_caves)). The two `mesa AND plateau(AND stone)` conditions are the vanilla **wolf and armadillo of Wooded Badlands**: `minecraft:mesa_plateau_stone` is fought over by our two badlands biomes (collision), and neither `pw:amber_wooded_badlands` nor `pw:badlands_mesa` carries `plateau` / `stone`, so those two vanilla conditions only survive in whatever sliver of vanilla Wooded Badlands is left. The rest wait on `sulfur_caves` / `dappled_forest` existing in his game version.

### 3.4 Block filters: unknown and legacy ids

| block id | status | conditions | rules (examples) | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `minecraft:leaves` | legacy | 61 | `pw:monkey_ws`, `sf_nba:bluejay`, `sf_nba:canary`, `sf_nba:cardinal` +10 | flattened to *_leaves |
| `minecraft:leaves2` | legacy | 60 | `sf_nba:bluejay`, `sf_nba:canary`, `sf_nba:cardinal`, `sf_nba:crow` +9 | flattened to acacia/dark_oak_leaves |
| `minecraft:log` | legacy | 46 | `sf_nba:bluejay`, `sf_nba:canary`, `sf_nba:cardinal`, `sf_nba:crow` +6 | flattened to *_log |
| `minecraft:log2` | legacy | 46 | `sf_nba:bluejay`, `sf_nba:canary`, `sf_nba:cardinal`, `sf_nba:crow` +6 | flattened to acacia/dark_oak_log |
| `minecraft:grass` | legacy | 20 | `minecraft:rabbit`, `minecraft:zombie_horse`, `sf_nba:duck`, `sf_nba:goose` | minecraft:grass_block (renamed 1.20.x) |
| `4ks_bop:orange_sand` | unknown | 4 | `sf_nba:fennec_fox`, `sf_nba:komodo_dragon`, `sf_nba:rattlesnake`, `sf_nba:termite` | not a vanilla 1.26.50 block and not one of our 467 custom blocks (missing mod) |

Unnamespaced ids (`dirt`, `grass`, `mud`, `water`, `sand`, `podzol` - 14 rules of one source) default to `minecraft:` and are fine except `grass` (legacy). `minecraft:air` in a surface filter can never be the block under a surface spawn; it is ignored as an entry. Every legacy entry above sits in a list that also names `minecraft:grass_block` or other valid blocks, so no rule is killed by them - but the leaf/log branch of those bird rules (spawning on tree tops) is probably dead.

### 3.5 Other rule/entity findings

- **is_spawnable** in an entity description only decides whether a spawn egg exists; it does not block natural spawning (123 StripMine entities have `false`, all still evaluated).
- **StripMine `scripts/entity/DespawnRule.js`** triggers `sf_nba:natural_despawn` 2 ticks after every natural sf_nba spawn; in 105/107 entities that event only adds `minecraft:despawn {despawn_from_distance}` (normal distance despawn) - **not a blocker**. Lions add it only when not sheared.
- **Entity-side kills at spawn:** no rule-bearing entity removes or transforms itself on `minecraft:entity_spawned` with certainty (simulated through every add/trigger/sequence/randomize). `sf_nba:firefly` has a 0.1 s `sf_nba:perish` timer group (not added at spawn by default).
- **BP-02 vanilla entity overrides (31 files):** scale-only differences against vanilla 1.26.50 (`minecraft:scale` values; sheep adds `pw:grass_block` eat pairs). Nothing spawn-related changed. Ten of them carry `format_version` 1.26.50 (axolotl, chicken, dolphin, endermite, fox, frog, horse, panda, polar_bear, rabbit) - if his game build is older than 1.26.50 the engine may refuse those files (vanilla definitions would then apply; spawning unaffected).
- **BP-02 rabbit override** (`bp02-205/spawn_rules/rabbit.json`) is built from an older vanilla rule: compared with vanilla 1.26.50 it drops the `dappled_forest` condition and moves desert rabbits to their own condition (weight 12, sand/red_sand/sandstone). Rabbits therefore never spawn in `dappled_forest` (if that biome exists in his version).
- **Pack precedence:** no spawn-rule or entity identifier is defined in both BP-02 and StripMine (checked). The 12 BP-02 spawn rules and 31 BP-02 entity files override vanilla files of the same path/identifier.
- **runtime_identifier `minecraft:pillager`** on `pw:gazelle_ysav`, `pw:gazelle_female_ysav`, `pw:giraffe_ysav`, `pw:hyena_ysav`, `pw:african_wild_dog_ysav` - KNOWLEDGE (unverified): on Peaceful difficulty the engine may remove them as hostile. Irrelevant on Easy+.
- **Vanilla data quirks (same in a vanilla world):** `minecraft:bee` spawn rule asks for tag `sunflower_plains`, which no vanilla biome carries (bees come from nests anyway); `minecraft:zombie_horse` filter is only the legacy id `minecraft:grass`; `minecraft:guardian` rule has no weight (monument spawns are engine-placed).
- **Structure-dependent vanilla mobs** (cats, villagers, iron golems, pillagers, witches in huts...): their natural appearance depends on structures generating in custom biomes (villages in `pw:desert_dunes` / `pw:boreal_forest`, swamp huts in `pw:swamp_lagoon`, pale oak creaking hearts in `pw:pale_hills`, which fully replaces `pale_garden`) - structure placement is engine code, **UNDECIDED statically**; plains / savanna / snowy plains villages are untouched.

### 5.2 Competition per water biome (underwater-capable rules only, max weight per rule)

Share = weight / total weight of all rules of that population that can fire there with `spawns_underwater` (the land-only rules of the same population in coastal parts of the biome compete too, so real shares are lower). `?` = only conditionally.

**`minecraft:beach`** (beach):

- animal - 3 rules, total weight 50: `crab` 21 (42%), `spotted_moray` 19 (38%), `glow_squid` 10 (20%)
- monster - 1 rules, total weight 0: `guardian` 0 (0%)?
- water_animal - 1 rules, total weight 6: `cavefish` 6 (100%)

**`minecraft:cold_beach`** (beach):

- animal - 2 rules, total weight 31: `crab` 21 (68%), `glow_squid` 10 (32%)
- monster - 1 rules, total weight 0: `guardian` 0 (0%)?
- water_animal - 1 rules, total weight 6: `cavefish` 6 (100%)

### 5.3 Why few aquatic mobs (mechanisms, ranked by static weight of evidence)

1. **Permanent cap fillers:** 12 sea creatures and 45 land animals have no `minecraft:despawn` (files under `stripmine-bp-1313/entities/pw_menagerie/wa|wwa|ws|ifs/`): `pw:african_elephant_wa`, `pw:anaconda_ws`, `pw:ant_wa`, `pw:asian_elephant_wa`, `pw:blue_jay_wa`, `pw:boar_ifs`, `pw:boar_wa`, `pw:capuchin_wa`, `pw:caracal_wa`, `pw:chimpanzee_wa`, `pw:cougar_wa`, `pw:crab_wa`, `pw:crocodile_wa`, `pw:deer_wa`, `pw:desert_owl_ws`, `pw:dove_wa`, `pw:duck_wa`, `pw:eagle_wa`, `pw:gelada_ws`, `pw:giant_squid_wwa`, `pw:gila_monster_ws`, `pw:giraffe_wa`, `pw:gorilla_wa`, `pw:hammerhead_shark_wa`, `pw:hedgehog_wa`, `pw:hornbill_ws`, `pw:komodo_dragon_wa`, `pw:lantern_fish_wa`, `pw:lion_wa`, `pw:lynx_ws`, `pw:monkey_ws`, `pw:orca_wa`, `pw:ostrich_wa`, `pw:ostrich_ws`, `pw:rat_wa`, `pw:rattlesnake_ws`, `pw:red_panda_wa`, `pw:rhino_wa`, `pw:shark_wa`, `pw:shrimp_wa`, `pw:snake_coral_wa`, `pw:snake_scarlet_wa`, `pw:snake_wa`, `pw:snow_leopard_wa`, `pw:stingray_wa`, `pw:swordfish_wa`, `pw:tapir_ws`, `pw:tiger_shark_wa`, `pw:tiger_wa`, `pw:tiger_ws`, `pw:toucan_wa`, `pw:turkey_wa`, `pw:whale_wa`, `pw:white_shark_wa`, `pw:white_tiger_wa`, `pw:yak_ws`, `pw:zebra_wa`.
   Effect: Every spawn is permanent and keeps occupying the "animal" population around the player. In a long-played area the cap stays full -> no new animal-group spawns at all (squid, dolphin, crab, seal, whales...). Fix: add `"minecraft:despawn": {"despawn_from_distance": {}}` to their `components` (same as AnF/sf_nba).
2. **Weight hogs:** `pw:shrimp_wa` and `pw:swordfish_wa` weight 300 each (`spawn_rules/pw_menagerie/wa/shrimp.json`, `.../swordfish.json` conditions[0].minecraft:weight) vs 2-30 for every other sea animal -> ~80% of ocean animal-group picks; shrimp herds 3-19 and are 0.25 blocks long (nearly invisible). Fix: weight 300 -> ~10, and exclude `deep` instead of `deep_ocean`.
3. **Wrong population:** big sea animals in "animal" compete with every land animal for one cap; moving them to `water_animal` (as vanilla fish and Naturalist fish are) separates the pools. Ruling needed (vanilla dolphins and squid also use "animal").
4. **Rules that cannot reach water:** 7 AnF sea animals are surface-only in oceans; electric eel / bass / catfish / BOP wetland conditions name tags that do not exist (section 3.2).
5. **No lakes, ponds not accepted:** inland water belongs to land biomes; almost no fish rule accepts them (only jungle and underground).
6. Not a cause (checked): our biomes (no ocean/beach replaced, river/swamp tags kept), sea-floor blocks (no water rule has a block filter), breathing (every underwater-spawning fish breathes water; air-breathers have 240 s supply like vanilla dolphins), height filters (all overlap the water column), StripMine `DespawnRule.js` (adds only normal distance despawn). Minor: `pw:otter_ytri` and `pw:anaconda_ws` can spawn underwater with `breathes_water` default false (15 s air) and walk navigation - may drown if they spawn deep.

## 8. Knowledge used that is NOT in any file (verify if a verdict hinges on it)

- Which vanilla biome ids the 1.18+ generator still places (section 1.3) - hills/mutated legacy ids treated as never generated.
- y-bands per placement (surface 62..320, river water 45..62, ocean water 25..62, deep ocean -10..62, underground -64..62) used only to test height_filter overlap.
- A `spawns_on_surface`-only rule cannot place a mob in ocean water (needs `spawns_underwater`) - flagged AT RISK, not NOT VIABLE.
- Bedrock entities without `minecraft:despawn` never despawn (section 5).
- The spawner picks among passing rules of a population in proportion to `weight`; population caps are engine constants (not in any file).
- Legacy block ids `minecraft:grass/leaves/leaves2/log/log2` are absent from the 1.26.50 block list; whether the engine still aliases them inside spawn filters is not documented - treated as "may not resolve".
